Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The universe variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 197 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 491 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the universe decomposition. By minimality, the element a^197 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 491-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
